- **pipeline-undo**: Undo a pipeline run's result. With worktree isolation (the current engine), this is clean and low-risk: a run never touches your checkout — its result lives only on a `pipeline/&lt;session&gt;` branch (and, for a `--push` run, on the remote). "Undo" therefore means deleting that branch and its worktree, not reverting your working tree.
